```cpp
#pragma once
// Stats (mockup 6A, "spotter's logbook"): today's count and busiest hour, the 24-hour chart, the top
// airlines and the types card. A vertical swipe switches to all-time: the top types and the all-time
// airline totals take the places of the chart and today's airlines. The text lives in the screen
// arena while the screen is up; regions are redrawn when their text changes.
#include "Screen.h"
#include "ScreenArena.h"
#include "Theme.h"

namespace scr {

class StatsView : public gfx::Layer {
   public:
    static constexpr gfx::Rect kHeader{0, 0, 240, 66}, kChart{0, 66, 240, 100}, kList{0, 166, 240, 100},
        kCard{0, 266, 240, 46};
    static constexpr int kRows = StatsSummary::kTop;
    explicit StatsView(const Theme& t) : t_(t) {}
    void attach(ScreenArena& a) { text_ = a.claim<Text>(this); }
    void setScene(const Scene* s) { scene_ = s, dirty_ = true; }
    void reset() { full_ = dirty_ = true, allTime_ = false; }
    void toggle() { allTime_ = !allTime_, dirty_ = true; }
    bool allTime() const { return allTime_; }
    void update(float dt, gfx::DamageList& damage) override;
    void draw(gfx::Canvas& c) override;

   private:
    struct Row {
        char name[24], count[12];
        gfx::Rgb colour;  // airline stripe (and bar, in a brand-fill theme)
        uint16_t frac;    // the bar's length, 0..1000 of the leader's
    };
    struct Text {
        char title[12], clock[8], count[16], unit[10], rightLabel[12], rightValue[24];
        bool chart;  // today's hour chart (the clock is valid)
        uint16_t bars[24], barMax;
        int hour;
        char chartTitle[20];  // all-time: the top types in the chart's place
        int chartRows;
        Row chartRow[kRows];
        char message[32];  // in the chart's place when there is neither
        char listTitle[24];
        int listRows;
        Row listRow[kRows];
        char listEmpty[32];
        char cardCount[8], cardNew[20], cardLine[64];
        bool cardNewUp;
    };
    void compose(Text& t) const;
    int rows(const StatsEntry* e, int n, bool airlines, Row* out) const;
    void drawRows(gfx::Canvas& c, const Row* r, int n, int y0, bool airlines) const;

    const Theme& t_;
    const Scene* scene_ = nullptr;
    Text* text_ = nullptr;
    bool full_ = true, dirty_ = true, allTime_ = false;
    uint32_t sig_[4] = {};
};

class StatsScreen : public Screen {
   public:
    StatsScreen(const Theme& t, ScreenArena& a) : view_(t), arena_(a) {}
    ScreenId id() const override { return ScreenId::Stats; }
    void setScene(const Scene* s) override { view_.setScene(s); }
    void enter() override { view_.attach(arena_), view_.reset(); }
    void onVerticalSwipe(int) override { view_.toggle(); }
    int layerCount() const override { return 1; }
    gfx::Layer* const* layers() override { return layers_; }

   private:
    StatsView view_;
    ScreenArena& arena_;
    gfx::Layer* layers_[1] = {&view_};
};

}  // namespace scr
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the screen.** Create `lib/core/screens/StatsScreen.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "StatsScreen.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

#include "data/AircraftTypes.h"
#include "data/Airlines.h"
#include "gfx/Font.h"
#include "gfx/Raster.h"
#include "gfx/fonts/Fonts.h"
#include "ui/Format.h"
#include "ui/Widgets.h"

namespace scr {
using namespace gfx;

namespace {
const char* const kMonths[] = {"Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"};

void formatDate(char* out, size_t n, int ymd) {  // 20260912 → "12 Sep 2026"
    int m = ymd / 100 % 100;
    std::snprintf(out, n, "%d %s %d", ymd % 100, kMonths[(m + 11) % 12], ymd / 10000);
}
}  // namespace

int StatsView::rows(const StatsEntry* e, int n, bool airlines, Row* out) const {
    const uint32_t lead = n ? e[0].count : 1;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        Row& r = out[i];
        const char* name = e[i].code;
        r.colour = t_.statsBar;
        if (airlines) {
            const data::Airline* al = data::airlineByCode(e[i].code);
            if (al) name = al->name;
            r.colour = al ? al->color : t_.brandNeutral;
        } else if (const char* tn = data::aircraftTypeName(e[i].code)) {
            name = tn;
        }
        ui::ellipsize(r.name, sizeof r.name, kFontSmall, name, 88);
        ui::formatThousands(r.count, sizeof r.count, int32_t(e[i].count));
        r.frac = uint16_t(lead ? uint64_t(e[i].count) * 1000 / lead : 0);
    }
    return n;
}

void StatsView::compose(Text& t) const {
    static const StatsSummary kNone;
    const StatsSummary& s = scene_ ? scene_->stats : kNone;
    std::memset(&t, 0, sizeof t);  // padding too: the regions are hashed byte for byte
    if (scene_ && scene_->timeValid) ui::formatClock(t.clock, sizeof t.clock, scene_->epoch);
    if (!allTime_) {
        std::snprintf(t.title, sizeof t.title, "TODAY");
        ui::formatThousands(t.count, sizeof t.count, int32_t(s.flightsToday));
        std::snprintf(t.unit, sizeof t.unit, "%s", s.flightsToday == 1 ? "flight" : "flights");
        int best = -1;
        for (int h = 0; h < 24; ++h)
            if (s.hourly[h] && (best < 0 || s.hourly[h] > s.hourly[best])) best = h;
        if (s.valid && best >= 0) {
            std::snprintf(t.rightLabel, sizeof t.rightLabel, "Busiest");
            std::snprintf(t.rightValue, sizeof t.rightValue, "%02d:00 · %u", best, unsigned(s.hourly[best]));
        }
        t.chart = s.valid;
        if (t.chart) {
            std::memcpy(t.bars, s.hourly, sizeof t.bars);
            t.hour = s.hour, t.barMax = 1;
            for (uint16_t b : t.bars) t.barMax = std::max(t.barMax, b);
        } else {
            std::snprintf(t.message, sizeof t.message, "Waiting for the clock");
        }
        std::snprintf(t.listTitle, sizeof t.listTitle, "TOP AIRLINES TODAY");
        t.listRows = rows(s.airlinesToday, s.nAirlinesToday, true, t.listRow);
    } else {
        std::snprintf(t.title, sizeof t.title, "ALL-TIME");
        ui::formatThousands(t.count, sizeof t.count, int32_t(s.flightsAll));
        std::snprintf(t.unit, sizeof t.unit, "%s", s.flightsAll == 1 ? "flight" : "flights");
        if (s.startYmd) {
            std::snprintf(t.rightLabel, sizeof t.rightLabel, "Since");
            formatDate(t.rightValue, sizeof t.rightValue, s.startYmd);
        }
        std::snprintf(t.chartTitle, sizeof t.chartTitle, "TOP TYPES");
        t.chartRows = rows(s.typesAll, s.nTypesAll, false, t.chartRow);
        if (!t.chartRows) std::snprintf(t.message, sizeof t.message, "No types counted yet");
        std::snprintf(t.listTitle, sizeof t.listTitle, "AIRLINES ALL-TIME");
        t.listRows = rows(s.airlinesAll, s.nAirlinesAll, true, t.listRow);
    }
    if (!t.listRows) std::snprintf(t.listEmpty, sizeof t.listEmpty, "No flights counted yet");
    std::snprintf(t.cardCount, sizeof t.cardCount, "%d", s.typesEver);
    t.cardNewUp = s.typesNewToday > 0;
    if (t.cardNewUp) std::snprintf(t.cardNew, sizeof t.cardNew, "+%d today", s.typesNewToday);
    else std::snprintf(t.cardNew, sizeof t.cardNew, "none new today");
    if (s.newestType[0]) {
        const char* name = data::aircraftTypeName(s.newestType);
        char when[8], line[96];
        ui::formatClock(when, sizeof when, s.newestEpoch);
        const char* type = name ? name : s.newestType;
        if (s.newestCallsign[0]) std::snprintf(line, sizeof line, "Newest: %s · %s · %s", type, s.newestCallsign, when);
        else std::snprintf(line, sizeof line, "Newest: %s · %s", type, when);
        // Too long with the time: drop the time before trimming the type name.
        if (textWidth(kFontSmall, line) > 200 && s.newestCallsign[0])
            std::snprintf(line, sizeof line, "Newest: %s · %s", type, s.newestCallsign);
        ui::ellipsize(t.cardLine, sizeof t.cardLine, kFontSmall, line, 200);
    } else {
        std::snprintf(t.cardLine, sizeof t.cardLine, "No first sightings yet");
    }
}

void StatsView::update(float, DamageList& damage) {
    if (!text_) return;
    if (dirty_) {
        dirty_ = false;
        compose(*text_);
        const Text& t = *text_;
        auto rowsSig = [](const Row* r, int n, uint32_t h) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) h = ui::hashBytes(&r[i], sizeof r[i], h);
            return h;
        };
        const uint32_t sig[4] = {
            ui::hashStr(t.title, ui::hashStr(t.clock, ui::hashStr(t.count, ui::hashStr(t.unit,
                        ui::hashStr(t.rightLabel, ui::hashStr(t.rightValue)))))),
            ui::hashBytes(t.bars, sizeof t.bars, ui::hashStr(t.chartTitle, ui::hashStr(t.message,
                        rowsSig(t.chartRow, t.chartRows, uint32_t(t.chart) + uint32_t(t.hour) * 7 + t.barMax * 131u +
                                                             uint32_t(t.chartRows) * 100003u)))),
            ui::hashStr(t.listTitle, ui::hashStr(t.listEmpty, rowsSig(t.listRow, t.listRows, uint32_t(t.listRows)))),
            ui::hashStr(t.cardCount, ui::hashStr(t.cardNew, ui::hashStr(t.cardLine, t.cardNewUp ? 1u : 2u))),
        };
        const Rect boxes[4] = {kHeader, kChart, kList, kCard};
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
            if (!full_ && sig[i] != sig_[i]) damage.add(boxes[i]);
            sig_[i] = sig[i];
        }
    }
    if (full_) damage.add({0, 0, 240, 320});
    full_ = false;
}

void StatsView::drawRows(Canvas& c, const Row* r, int n, int y0, bool airlines) const {
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const int y = y0 + i * 14;
        int x = 14;
        if (airlines) ui::stripe(c, 12, y - 9, 11, r[i].colour), x = 22;
        drawText(c, kFontSmall, x, y, r[i].name, t_.text);
        const Rgb bar = airlines && t_.brandFill ? r[i].colour : t_.statsBar;
        c.fill({112, y - 6, 84, 4}, rgb565(t_.statsTrack));
        const int w = 84 * r[i].frac / 1000;
        if (w > 0) c.fill({112, y - 6, w, 4}, rgb565(bar));
        drawText(c, kFontSmall, 226, y, r[i].count, t_.textSoft, Align::Right);
    }
}

void StatsView::draw(Canvas& c) {
    if (!text_) return;
    const Text& t = *text_;
    Rgb bg[] = {t_.boardBgTop, t_.boardBgBottom};
    float pos[] = {0.f, 1.f};
    ui::vFill(c, {0, 0, 240, 320}, bg, pos, 2, t_);
    if (c.clip().intersects(kHeader)) {
        drawText(c, kFontSmall, 14, 20, t.title, t_.textSoft);
        drawText(c, kFontBody, 226, 20, t.clock, t_.textSoft, Align::Right);
        int x = drawText(c, *t_.fontDisplay, 14, 58, t.count, t_.text);
        // The unit only where it clears the right-hand value ("18,420" next to "12 Sep 2026" does not).
        int room = 226 - (t.rightValue[0] ? textWidth(kFontBody, t.rightValue) + 6 : 0);
        if (x + 5 + textWidth(kFontSmall, t.unit) <= room) drawText(c, kFontSmall, x + 5, 58, t.unit, t_.textDim);
        if (t.rightLabel[0]) {
            drawText(c, kFontSmall, 226, 40, t.rightLabel, t_.textDim, Align::Right);
            drawText(c, kFontBody, 226, 58, t.rightValue, t_.text, Align::Right);
        }
    }
    if (c.clip().intersects(kChart)) {
        if (t.chart) {
            constexpr int kBase = 150, kMaxH = 70;
            for (int h = 0; h < 24; ++h) {
                const int x = 12 + h * 9;
                c.fill({x, kBase, 7, 2}, rgb565(h > t.hour ? t_.statsFuture : t_.statsTrack));  // the axis
                const int bh = t.bars[h] ? std::max(2, t.bars[h] * kMaxH / t.barMax) : 0;
                if (!bh) continue;
                const Rgb col = h < t.hour ? t_.statsBar : h == t.hour ? t_.statsNow : t_.statsFuture;
                fillRoundRect(c, {x, kBase - bh, 7, bh}, t_.flat ? 0.f : 2.f, col);
            }
            char l[4];
            for (int h = 0; h < 24; h += 6)
                std::snprintf(l, sizeof l, "%02d", h), drawText(c, kFontSmall, 12 + h * 9, 163, l, t_.textDim);
        } else if (t.chartRows) {
            drawText(c, kFontSmall, 14, 82, t.chartTitle, t_.textDim);
            drawRows(c, t.chartRow, t.chartRows, 98, false);
        } else {
            drawText(c, kFontBody, 120, 120, t.message, t_.textDim, Align::Center);
        }
    }
    if (c.clip().intersects(kList)) {
        drawText(c, kFontSmall, 14, 182, t.listTitle, t_.textDim);
        if (t.listRows) drawRows(c, t.listRow, t.listRows, 198, true);
        else drawText(c, kFontBody, 120, 222, t.listEmpty, t_.textDim, Align::Center);
    }
    if (c.clip().intersects(kCard)) {
        ui::panel(c, {10, 268, 220, 42}, 10, t_.statsCard, t_.statsCardEdge, t_);
        int x = drawText(c, kFontSmall, 20, 284, "TYPES SEEN", t_.textDim);
        drawText(c, *t_.fontLabel, x + 6, 285, t.cardCount, t_.text);
        drawText(c, kFontSmall, 220, 284, t.cardNew, t.cardNewUp ? t_.climb : t_.textDim, Align::Right);
        drawText(c, kFontSmall, 20, 302, t.cardLine, t_.textSoft);
    }
}

}  // namespace scr
```


  - replace `main()` with:

```python
def main():
    only = set(sys.argv[1:])
    unknown = only - {f[0] for f in FONTS}
    if unknown:
        sys.exit("unknown font: " + ", ".join(sorted(unknown)))
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    decls = "\n".join(f"extern const FontData {f[0]};" for f in FONTS)
    (OUT / "Fonts.h").write_text(
        "// Generated by tools/gen_font.py. Do not edit.\n#pragma once\n"
        '#include "../Font.h"\n\nnamespace gfx {\n' + decls + "\n}  // namespace gfx\n"
    )
    total = sum(gen(*f) for f in FONTS if not only or f[0] in only)
    print(f"~{total} bytes of flash for the fonts generated")
```

```cpp
        {255, 210, 0},                                       // radar blip core
        {0, 0, 0}, {245, 245, 245}, {245, 245, 245}, {255, 60, 60}, {255, 210, 0},  // tree, moon, glow, beacon, home
        {255, 200, 80}, {255, 220, 120}, {255, 236, 160},    // sun

        {24, 24, 28}, {200, 120, 150},                       // airglow, belt of Venus
        &gfx::kFontCondHero, &gfx::kFontCondTitle, &gfx::kFontCondLabel,
        true, true, true,                                    // flat; airline fills; capital headings

        {255, 150, 70}, {120, 220, 140}, {170, 210, 255},    // (planes take airline colours here)

        {200, 200, 206}, {255, 210, 0}, {50, 50, 56}, {38, 38, 42}, {18, 18, 20}, {70, 70, 76},
    };
    return t;
}
```

```cpp
        if (fw > 0) c.fill({x, y - 1, fw, 3}, rgb565(fill));
        return;
    }
```

  - `statTile`: draw the box only when rounded: `if (!t.flat) fillRoundRect(c, r, 8, t.tile);`
  - `compass`: replace its body with:

```cpp
    // Flat: a white disc with dark marks (Bold Editorial); else a dim disc with a ring.
    const bool flat = t.flat;
    const Rgb ink = flat ? t.card : t.textSoft, arrow = flat ? t.card : t.accent;
    aaCircle(c, cx, cy, r, flat ? t.text : t.veil, flat ? 255 : 140);
    if (!flat) aaRing(c, cx, cy, r - 0.5f, 1, t.textSoft, 150);
    // Ticks at E, S and W; north gets its letter.
    for (int k = 1; k < 4; ++k) {
        float a = k * 90 * 3.14159265f / 180.f, s = std::sin(a), co = -std::cos(a);
        aaLine(c, cx + s * (r - 4), cy + co * (r - 4), cx + s * (r - 1.5f), cy + co * (r - 1.5f), 1, ink, 140);
    }
    drawText(c, kFontSmall, int(cx), int(cy - r + 11), "N", ink, Align::Center, 200);
    float a = bearingDeg * 3.14159265f / 180.f, s = std::sin(a), co = std::cos(a);
    auto rot = [&](float px, float py) { return Vec2{cx + px * co - py * s, cy + px * s + py * co}; };
    // Arrowhead pointing "up" in its own frame, rotated to the bearing; short enough to clear
    // the N unless it points almost straight at it.
    Vec2 pts[] = {rot(0, -(r - 6)), rot(4.5f, 4.5f), rot(0, 1.5f), rot(-4.5f, 4.5f)};
    aaPolygon(c, pts, 4, arrow);
```

  The non-flat branch draws exactly what it drew before, so Cinematic goldens do not move.

```cpp
void ClosestScreen::refresh() {
    const SceneFlight* f = nullptr;
    int index = 0;
    if (scene_ && scene_->count) {
        f = selected_ ? scene_->find(selected_) : nullptr;
        if (!f) f = &scene_->flights[0];
        index = int(f - scene_->flights);
    }
    // Bold Editorial: the band is a flat field in the plane's airline colour (neutral when unknown).
    const data::Airline* al = f ? data::airlineForCallsign(f->callsign) : nullptr;
    sky_.setFlat(t_.brandFill, al ? al->color : t_.brandNeutral);
    if (scene_) {
        sky_.setSun(scene_->sunElev, scene_->sunAz);
        float sx, sy;
        bool sun = sky_.sunPoint(sx, sy) && sky_.sunDiscVisible() && sy < InfoCard::kTop;  // a visible disc
        plane_.setAvoid(sun, sx, sy);
    }
    plane_.setFlight(f);
    card_.set(scene_, f, index);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** In `test/test_director/test_main.cpp`, replace `test_radar_and_board_alternate` (and its `RUN_TEST`) with the tests below, change `test_carousel_and_stay`'s third assertion to expect `ScreenId::Map` at 60000 (the carousel is now the six-screen swipe order), and `RUN_TEST` every new test:

```cpp
static void test_planes_rotate_radar_map_board() {
    Director d;
    ScreenId cur = ScreenId::Radar;
    run(d, scene(5), cur, 0);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Radar), int(run(d, scene(5), cur, 44000)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Map), int(run(d, scene(5), cur, 45000)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Board), int(run(d, scene(5), cur, 90000)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Radar), int(run(d, scene(5), cur, 135000)));
}

static void test_no_planes_alternates_sky_and_stats() {
    Director d;
    ScreenId cur = ScreenId::Radar;
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Sky), int(run(d, scene(0), cur, 0)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Sky), int(run(d, scene(0), cur, 44000)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Stats), int(run(d, scene(0), cur, 45000)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Sky), int(run(d, scene(0), cur, 90000)));
}

static scr::Scene sighted(int n, uint32_t seq) {
    scr::Scene s = scene(n);
    s.stats.firstSeq = seq;
    return s;
}

static void test_first_sighting_shows_stats_for_twenty_seconds() {
    Director d;
    ScreenId cur = ScreenId::Radar;
    run(d, sighted(3, 5), cur, 0);  // the count at boot is not news
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Radar), int(run(d, sighted(3, 5), cur, 25000)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Stats), int(run(d, sighted(3, 6), cur, 26000)));  // a new type
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Stats), int(run(d, sighted(3, 6), cur, 45000)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Radar), int(run(d, sighted(3, 6), cur, 46001)));
}

static void test_first_sighting_waits_for_the_dwell() {
    Director d;
    ScreenId cur = ScreenId::Radar;
    run(d, sighted(3, 1), cur, 0);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Radar), int(run(d, sighted(3, 2), cur, 5000)));  // the radar's 20 s first
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Stats), int(run(d, sighted(3, 2), cur, 20000)));
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Stats), int(run(d, sighted(3, 2), cur, 39999)));  // 20 s from when it came up
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Radar), int(run(d, sighted(3, 2), cur, 40000)));
}

static void test_one_sighting_takeover_per_five_minutes() {
    Director d;
    ScreenId cur = ScreenId::Radar;
    run(d, sighted(3, 1), cur, 0);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Stats), int(run(d, sighted(3, 2), cur, 20000)));
    run(d, sighted(3, 2), cur, 40000);  // back to the rotation
    TEST_ASSERT_TRUE(run(d, sighted(3, 3), cur, 100000) != ScreenId::Stats);  // too soon after the last
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Stats), int(run(d, sighted(3, 4), cur, 341000)));
}

static void test_a_close_plane_beats_a_sighting() {
    Director d;
    ScreenId cur = ScreenId::Radar;
    run(d, sighted(3, 1), cur, 0);
    scr::Scene s = sighted(3, 2);
    s.flights[0].distNm = 2, s.flights[0].altFt = 8000;
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(int(ScreenId::Closest), int(run(d, s, cur, 20000)));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to see them fail.** Run `pio test -e test -f test_director`. Expected: the rotation, idle, sighting and carousel tests fail (the director still alternates Radar and Board).

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite the director's rules.** Replace `Director.h` with:

```cpp
#pragma once
// Chooses the screen when nobody is touching the device. Rules (spec §4), in order: no location or
// clock → Radar; nothing in range → Sky View and Stats, 45 s each; a close, low plane → Closest
// Flight; a type seen for the first time → Stats for 20 s (at most once every 5 minutes); a plane
// high overhead when the sky is interesting → Sky View; otherwise Radar, Map and Board, 45 s each.
// A screen stays at least 20 s, thresholds release 20 % past where they trigger, and a touch hands
// control to the user for a minute (explore mode).
#include "Scene.h"
#include "Screen.h"

namespace scr {

enum class DirectorMode : uint8_t { Auto, Carousel, Stay };
// The screen_mode setting ("auto", "carousel", "stay"); anything else is Auto.
DirectorMode parseDirectorMode(const char* setting);

class Director {
   public:
    static constexpr uint32_t kDwellMs = 20000, kExploreMs = 60000, kAlternateMs = 45000;
    static constexpr uint32_t kSightingMs = 20000, kSightingGapMs = 300000;
    static constexpr float kCloseNm = 3, kCloseFt = 15000, kHighDeg = 45, kRelease = 1.2f;

    void configure(DirectorMode mode, uint32_t carouselMs) { mode_ = mode, carouselMs_ = carouselMs; }
    void noteTouch(uint32_t now) { lastTouch_ = now, touched_ = true; }
    bool exploring(uint32_t now) const { return touched_ && now - lastTouch_ < kExploreMs; }
    // The screen that should be showing now. `current` is where the host is heading (its
    // target, not the screen it may still be fading out of); the director adopts it while
    // the user is in charge.
    ScreenId decide(const Scene& s, ScreenId current, uint32_t now);

   private:
    ScreenId rule(const Scene& s, uint32_t now);
    void noteSightings(const Scene& s, uint32_t now);
    // A rotation (Radar → Map → Board, or Sky ↔ Stats), 45 s a screen; a new list starts at its first.
    ScreenId rotate(const ScreenId* list, int n, uint32_t now);

    DirectorMode mode_ = DirectorMode::Auto;
    uint32_t carouselMs_ = 30000;
    bool started_ = false;
    // What we last asked for. Holding it (not the host's current screen) keeps a switch
    // going while the host is still fading out of the old screen.
    ScreenId asked_ = ScreenId::Count;
    uint32_t shownSince_ = 0;  // when we last asked for a different screen
    bool closeLatched_ = false, skyLatched_ = false;
    const ScreenId* rot_ = nullptr;  // the rotation running; null while a rule holds the screen
    int rotN_ = 0, rotIdx_ = 0;
    uint32_t rotSince_ = 0;
    // First sightings, from Scene::stats.firstSeq.
    bool seqInit_ = false, sighting_ = false, sightingShown_ = false, anySighting_ = false;
    uint32_t seq_ = 0, sightingFrom_ = 0, lastSighting_ = 0;
    uint32_t lastTouch_ = 0;
    bool touched_ = false;
};

}  // namespace scr
```

and `Director.cpp` with:

```cpp
#include "Director.h"

#include <cstring>

namespace scr {

namespace {
// Carousel order: the swipe order.
const ScreenId kCycle[] = {ScreenId::Closest, ScreenId::Radar, ScreenId::Map, ScreenId::Board, ScreenId::Sky,
                           ScreenId::Stats};
constexpr int kCycleN = sizeof(kCycle) / sizeof(kCycle[0]);
const ScreenId kBusy[] = {ScreenId::Radar, ScreenId::Map, ScreenId::Board};
const ScreenId kIdle[] = {ScreenId::Sky, ScreenId::Stats};
}  // namespace

DirectorMode parseDirectorMode(const char* setting) {
    if (!setting) return DirectorMode::Auto;
    if (!std::strcmp(setting, "carousel")) return DirectorMode::Carousel;
    if (!std::strcmp(setting, "stay")) return DirectorMode::Stay;
    return DirectorMode::Auto;
}

ScreenId Director::rotate(const ScreenId* list, int n, uint32_t now) {
    if (rot_ != list) rot_ = list, rotN_ = n, rotIdx_ = 0, rotSince_ = now;
    else if (now - rotSince_ >= kAlternateMs) rotIdx_ = (rotIdx_ + 1) % rotN_, rotSince_ = now;
    return rot_[rotIdx_];
}

void Director::noteSightings(const Scene& s, uint32_t now) {
    const uint32_t seq = s.stats.firstSeq;
    if (!seqInit_) {  // the count at boot is not news
        seqInit_ = true, seq_ = seq;
        return;
    }
    if (seq == seq_) return;
    seq_ = seq;
    // A new type: Stats for a while, unless one took over in the last five minutes (a new logbook
    // sees a new type with almost every plane).
    if (!sighting_ && (!anySighting_ || now - lastSighting_ >= kSightingGapMs)) sighting_ = true, sightingShown_ = false;
}

ScreenId Director::rule(const Scene& s, uint32_t now) {
    noteSightings(s, now);
    if (sighting_ && sightingShown_ && now - sightingFrom_ >= kSightingMs)
        sighting_ = false, anySighting_ = true, lastSighting_ = now;
    if (!s.locationSet || !s.timeValid) return rot_ = nullptr, ScreenId::Radar;
    if (s.count == 0) return closeLatched_ = skyLatched_ = false, rotate(kIdle, 2, now);
    // Once a rule holds, it lets go only 20 % past where it took hold, so a plane hovering
    // at the threshold does not flip the screen back and forth.
    float closeNm = closeLatched_ ? kCloseNm * kRelease : kCloseNm;
    float closeFt = closeLatched_ ? kCloseFt * kRelease : kCloseFt;
    float highDeg = skyLatched_ ? kHighDeg / kRelease : kHighDeg;
    bool close = false, high = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < s.count; ++i) {
        const SceneFlight& f = s.flights[i];
        if (f.distNm <= closeNm && f.altFt <= closeFt) close = true;
        if (f.elevDeg >= highDeg) high = true;
    }
    // Dusk, night or the moon up: the sky itself is worth looking at.
    bool interesting = s.sunElev < 6 || s.moonElev > 0;
    closeLatched_ = close, skyLatched_ = high && interesting;
    if (close) return rot_ = nullptr, ScreenId::Closest;
    if (sighting_) return rot_ = nullptr, ScreenId::Stats;
    if (skyLatched_) return rot_ = nullptr, ScreenId::Sky;
    return rotate(kBusy, 3, now);
}

ScreenId Director::decide(const Scene& s, ScreenId current, uint32_t now) {
    // The user is in charge: follow whatever they chose.
    if (mode_ == DirectorMode::Stay || exploring(now)) return asked_ = current;
    if (!started_) {  // the first decision after boot applies at once
        started_ = true, shownSince_ = now;
        return asked_ = mode_ == DirectorMode::Carousel ? current : rule(s, now);
    }
    ScreenId hold = asked_, want = hold;
    if (mode_ == DirectorMode::Carousel) {
        if (now - shownSince_ < carouselMs_) return hold;
        for (int i = 0; i < kCycleN; ++i)
            if (kCycle[i] == hold) want = kCycle[(i + 1) % kCycleN];
    } else {
        want = rule(s, now);
        if (want != hold && now - shownSince_ < kDwellMs) return hold;
    }
    if (want != hold) shownSince_ = now;
    asked_ = want;
    // A first sighting's 20 s count from when Stats actually came up (the dwell may delay it).
    if (sighting_ && !sightingShown_ && asked_ == ScreenId::Stats) sightingShown_ = true, sightingFrom_ = now;
    return asked_;
}

}  // namespace scr
```

```cpp
// The Live Map's mask rebuild and airport query (on entering the map, or a new home or radius): at
// San Francisco and around New York, where the coastline is busiest. Target ≤ 60 ms.
void mapMaskBench() {
    static uint8_t bits[basemap::LandMask::kBytes];
    static basemap::AirportMark marks[scr::MapBase::kMaxMarks];
    basemap::LandMask m(bits);
    struct Place {
        const char* name;
        double lat, lon;
    };
    for (const Place& p : {Place{"SFO", 37.6189, -122.375}, Place{"JFK", 40.6413, -73.7781}})
        for (float r : {5.f, 25.f, 50.f, 100.f}) {
            basemap::MapView v = basemap::viewFor(p.lat, p.lon, r);
            uint32_t t = micros();
            int tiles = m.build(v);
            uint32_t maskUs = micros() - t;
            t = micros();
            int n = basemap::airportsInView(v, marks, scr::MapBase::kMaxMarks);
            basemap::placeLabels(marks, n, kFontSmall, 240);
            uint32_t apUs = micros() - t;
            Serial.printf("[map] %s %3.0f nm: mask %d tiles %6u us, %2d airports %5u us\n", p.name, r, tiles, maskUs, n,
                          apUs);
        }
}
```
